    {
      "path": "packages/operator-core/lib/work-items.ts",
      "sha256": "75d40cfbd5d8eb2cd19e2707ee28999dde1ff1d786fc912515b75d2a7e178967",
      "excerptStartLine": 7597,
      "excerptEndLine": 7611,
      "excerpt": " * EI-266267: an active original-item admission recovery is a reservation while its\n * promoter is screening or waiting to assign the requested target. The request is\n * deliberately kept in the item payload, so ordinary self-select must exclude its\n * queued/held states while a named target claim remains available. `delivered` is\n * intentionally fail-open: the assignment path has already handed the item to the\n * target (and the normal taken-by floor protects it), while a stale delivery must not\n * permanently starve the queue.\n */\nexport function admissionRecoveryExclusionSql(\n  sql: OrgSql,\n  payloadCol: 'wi.payload' | 'payload' | 'ei.payload' | 'target.payload' = 'wi.payload',\n) {\n  return sql`COALESCE(${sql.unsafe(payloadCol)}, '{}'::jsonb) #>> '{_admissionRecovery,state}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'queued'\n    AND COALESCE(${sql.unsafe(payloadCol)}, '{}'::jsonb) #>> '{_admissionRecovery,state}' IS DISTINCT FROM 'held'`;\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "packages/operator-core/lib/work-items.ts",
      "sha256": "75d40cfbd5d8eb2cd19e2707ee28999dde1ff1d786fc912515b75d2a7e178967",
      "excerptStartLine": 7641,
      "excerptEndLine": 7649,
      "excerpt": "/** JS twin of {@link admissionRecoveryExclusionSql} for claimable-event guards. */\nexport function isAdmissionRecoveryReserved(payload: unknown): boolean {\n  if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object') return false;\n  const recovery = (payload as Record<string, unknown>)._admissionRecovery;\n  if (!recovery || typeof recovery !== 'object') return false;\n  const state = (recovery as Record<string, unknown>).state;\n  return state === 'queued' || state === 'held';\n}\n\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "packages/operator-core/lib/work-item-admission-recovery.ts",
      "sha256": "21ca16967530c2d14c9385331f9ebb5266a8b2d2a257645e460390f3dbafb9f7",
      "excerptStartLine": 29,
      "excerptEndLine": 73,
      "excerpt": "const pickupObservationSchema = z.object({\n  requestId: z.string().uuid(),\n  workItemId: z.string().min(1),\n  target: z.string().min(1),\n  deliveredAt: z.string(),\n  observedAt: z.string(),\n  verdict: pickupVerdictSchema,\n  activityAt: z.string().nullable(),\n  source: z.literal('last-progress-at').nullable(),\n  reason: z.string().min(1).max(1000),\n});\nconst requestSchema = z.object({\n  version: z.union([z.literal(1), z.literal(2), z.literal(3)]),\n  requestId: z.string().uuid(),\n  caller: z.string().min(1),\n  target: z.string().min(1),\n  routineId: z.string().min(1),\n  signature: z.string().regex(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/),\n  state: z.enum(['queued', 'delivered', 'held']),\n  requestedAt: z.string(),\n  updatedAt: z.string(),\n  reason: z.string().optional(),\n  /** Last observed stage, never a grant or proof that the target executed. */\n  stage: recoveryStageSchema.optional(),\n  /** Only a later target-owned pickup can establish execution. */\n  pickupConfirmed: z.literal(false),\n  /** Delivery instant for the most recent successfully queued wake. */\n  deliveredAt: z.string().optional(),\n  /** Separate, later observation; never a wake-fan return value. */\n  pickupObservation: pickupObservationSchema.optional(),\n  note: z.string().min(1).max(DEFAULT_INBOX_BODY_CAP).optional(),\n  body: z.string().max(DEFAULT_INBOX_BODY_CAP).optional(),\n  authority: admissionAuthoritySchema.optional(),\n}).refine((r) => r.version !== 1 || (r.note === undefined && r.body === undefined), {\n  message: 'v1 recovery requests cannot carry unsigned instructions',\n}).refine((r) => r.version === 3 || r.authority === undefined, {\n  message: 'legacy recovery requests cannot carry unsigned authority',\n}).refine((r) => r.version !== 3 || r.authority !== undefined, {\n  message: 'v3 recovery requests require authenticated authority',\n}).refine((r) => r.pickupObservation === undefined || r.pickupObservation.requestId === r.requestId, {\n  message: 'pickup observation must match the immutable recovery request id',\n});\nexport type AdmissionRecoveryRequest = z.infer<typeof requestSchema>;\nexport type AdmissionRecoveryPickupVerdict = z.infer<typeof pickupVerdictSchema>;\nexport type AdmissionRecoveryPickupObservation = z.infer<typeof pickupObservationSchema>;\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "packages/operator-core/lib/work-item-admission-recovery.ts",
      "sha256": "21ca16967530c2d14c9385331f9ebb5266a8b2d2a257645e460390f3dbafb9f7",
      "excerptStartLine": 128,
      "excerptEndLine": 165,
      "excerpt": "export async function authorizeAdmissionRecovery(\n  scope: AdmissionRecoveryScope,\n  request: Pick<AdmissionRecoveryRequest, 'caller' | 'target' | 'authority' | 'note' | 'body'>,\n): Promise<void> {\n  for (const actor of new Set([request.caller, request.target])) {\n    const presence = await getPresence(actor);\n    if (!presence || presence.revoked || presence.workspaceId !== scope.workspaceId) {\n      throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused(`actor ${actor} is absent, revoked, or outside this workspace`);\n    }\n    if ((await getModes(scope.workspaceId, actor)).some((mode) => mode.mode === AUDIT_MODE)) {\n      throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused(auditModeMutationDenyReason({ toolName: 'work_items:claim', ownerId: actor }));\n    }\n    // A system routine must not launder a session's launch-declared denial.\n    // Reuse the canonical state/evaluator, with a fresh read rather than its UI cache.\n    const confinements = await readOperatorState<SessionConfinementsPayload>(\n      'operator_session_confinements', scope.workspaceId, { fresh: true },\n    );\n    const confinement = confinements?.sessions?.[actor];\n    if (confinement) {\n      const expiry = Date.parse(confinement.expiresAt);\n      if (!Number.isFinite(expiry)) throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused(`${actor}: confinement expiry is invalid`);\n      if (expiry > Date.now()) {\n        for (const toolName of actor === request.caller ? ['coord:dispatch', 'work_items:claim'] : ['work_items:claim']) {\n          const verdict = evaluateSessionConfinement({ toolName, confinement: confinement.confinement });\n          if (!verdict.allowed) throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused(`${actor}: ${verdict.reason}`);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    const permission = await authorizeWorkItemDispatch({\n      workItemId: scope.workItemId, target: actor, harness: scope.harnessSlug, workspaceId: scope.workspaceId,\n    });\n    if (!permission.allowed) throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused(`${actor}: ${permission.reason}`);\n  }\n  try {\n    await authorizeAdmissionReplay(getOrgPg().sql, scope, request);\n  } catch (error) {\n    if (error instanceof AdmissionAuthorityRefused) throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused(error.message);\n    throw error;\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "packages/operator-core/lib/work-item-admission-recovery.ts",
      "sha256": "21ca16967530c2d14c9385331f9ebb5266a8b2d2a257645e460390f3dbafb9f7",
      "excerptStartLine": 169,
      "excerptEndLine": 208,
      "excerpt": "function savedRequest(item: RecoveryItem): AdmissionRecoveryRequest | null {\n  const value = item.payload?._admissionRecovery;\n  if (value == null) return null;\n  const parsed = requestSchema.safeParse(value);\n  if (!parsed.success) throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('malformed original-item recovery request; retry through the authorized request door');\n  return parsed.data;\n}\n\n/** Payload is caller-writable on feature creation. Bind provenance to the existing\n * operator key, domain-separated from spawn tokens; a claimed caller name is not proof. */\nasync function requestSignature(sql: Sql, scope: AdmissionRecoveryScope, request: Omit<AdmissionRecoveryRequest, 'signature'>): Promise<string> {\n  const [key] = await sql<{ value_b64: string }[]>`\n    SELECT value_b64 FROM harness_shared.operator_secrets WHERE name = 'spawn-signing-key'`;\n  if (!key) throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('operator signing key unavailable; recovery was not authorized');\n  return createHmac('sha256', decodeOperatorSecretKey(key.value_b64, 'spawn-signing-key'))\n    .update(JSON.stringify([`admission-recovery-v${request.version}`, scope.workspaceId, scope.harnessSlug, scope.workItemId,\n      request.requestId, request.caller, request.target, request.routineId, request.requestedAt,\n      ...(request.version >= 2 ? [request.note ?? null, request.body ?? null] : []),\n      // Parsing fixes property order across input objects and PG jsonb reads.\n      ...(request.version === 3 ? [admissionAuthoritySchema.parse(request.authority)] : [])]))\n    .digest('hex');\n}\n\nasync function verifyRequest(sql: Sql, scope: AdmissionRecoveryScope, request: AdmissionRecoveryRequest): Promise<void> {\n  const expected = await requestSignature(sql, scope, request);\n  if (!timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(expected, 'hex'), Buffer.from(request.signature, 'hex'))) {\n    throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('recovery provenance is invalid for this exact item and caller');\n  }\n}\n\n/** Signed prior placement is evidence for review, never admission or execution. */\nexport async function readVerifiedAdmissionRecoveryTarget(\n  scope: AdmissionRecoveryScope,\n  sql: Sql = getOrgPg().sql,\n): Promise<string | null> {\n  const request = savedRequest(await readItem(sql, scope));\n  if (!request || request.version !== 3 || request.state !== 'held' || request.stage !== 'screen') return null;\n  await verifyRequest(sql, scope, request);\n  return request.target;\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "packages/operator-core/lib/work-item-admission-recovery.ts",
      "sha256": "21ca16967530c2d14c9385331f9ebb5266a8b2d2a257645e460390f3dbafb9f7",
      "excerptStartLine": 239,
      "excerptEndLine": 278,
      "excerpt": "\nfunction assertItemEligible(item: RecoveryItem, target: string): void {\n  if (!['open', 'todo', 'failing', 'wip', 'in_progress', 'validating'].includes(item.status)) {\n    throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused(`original item is ${item.status}; recovery does not reopen it`);\n  }\n  if (item.origin !== 'local') throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('remote work must be repaired by its originating actor');\n  if (item.taken_by && item.taken_by !== target) throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused(`item is held by ${item.taken_by}`);\n  if (item.payload?._claimHold === true || item.payload?.needsOwnerAction === true) {\n    throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('original item has an owner-action or claim hold');\n  }\n}\n\nasync function assertRoutineActive(sql: Sql, scope: AdmissionRecoveryScope, id?: string): Promise<string> {\n  const rows = await sql<{ id: string }[]>`\n    SELECT id FROM harness_shared.routines\n     WHERE workspace_id = ${scope.workspaceId} AND install_slug = ${scope.harnessSlug}\n       AND name = ${WORK_ITEM_ADMISSION_PROMOTER}\n       AND target_role = ${`system:${WORK_ITEM_ADMISSION_PROMOTER}`} AND active = true\n       AND (trigger_config->>'cron' IS NOT NULL OR trigger_config->>'rrule' IS NOT NULL)\n       AND (${id ?? null}::text IS NULL OR id = ${id ?? null})`;\n  if (rows.length !== 1) throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('the existing recurring admission promoter must be present and active');\n  return rows[0]!.id;\n}\n\n/** Advisory locks serialize our consumers, not other writers of the public payload.\n * Compare the complete saved request so an old worker cannot overwrite a replacement. */\nasync function writeRequest(\n  sql: Sql, scope: AdmissionRecoveryScope, request: Record<string, unknown>, expected: unknown,\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  const rows = await sql`\n    UPDATE harness_shared.work_items\n       SET payload = jsonb_set(COALESCE(payload, '{}'::jsonb), '{_admissionRecovery}',\n         ${sql.typed(JSON.stringify(request), 25)}::jsonb, true)\n     WHERE workspace_id = ${scope.workspaceId} AND harness_slug = ${scope.harnessSlug}\n       AND feature_id = ${scope.workItemId}\n       AND COALESCE(payload->'_admissionRecovery', 'null'::jsonb) =\n         ${sql.typed(JSON.stringify(expected ?? null), 25)}::jsonb\n     RETURNING feature_id`;\n  return rows.length === 1;\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "packages/operator-core/lib/work-item-admission-recovery.ts",
      "sha256": "21ca16967530c2d14c9385331f9ebb5266a8b2d2a257645e460390f3dbafb9f7",
      "excerptStartLine": 287,
      "excerptEndLine": 338,
      "excerpt": "/** Pure classifier: a delivery receipt is not pickup; only this later target-owned\n * work-item progress observation can confirm pickup. */\nexport function classifyAdmissionRecoveryPickup(input: {\n  requestId: string;\n  workItemId: string;\n  target: string;\n  deliveredAt: string;\n  reading: AdmissionRecoveryActivityReading | null;\n  nowMs?: number;\n}): AdmissionRecoveryPickupObservation {\n  const observedAt = new Date(input.nowMs ?? Date.now()).toISOString();\n  const make = (\n    verdict: AdmissionRecoveryPickupVerdict,\n    activityAt: string | null,\n    source: AdmissionRecoveryPickupObservation['source'],\n    reason: string,\n  ): AdmissionRecoveryPickupObservation => ({\n    requestId: input.requestId,\n    workItemId: input.workItemId,\n    target: input.target,\n    deliveredAt: input.deliveredAt,\n    observedAt,\n    verdict,\n    activityAt,\n    source,\n    reason,\n  });\n  const deliveredMs = Date.parse(input.deliveredAt);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(deliveredMs)) {\n    return make('unreadable', null, null, 'recovery delivery instant is unreadable; pickup cannot be measured');\n  }\n  if (!input.reading) {\n    return make('unreadable', null, null, 'target work-item activity could not be read; measurement failed, not evidence of no pickup');\n  }\n  if (input.reading.assignee !== input.target) {\n    return make('not-yet', null, null, 'the target does not currently hold the original work item; target-owned pickup remains unconfirmed');\n  }\n  if (input.reading.lastProgressAt == null) {\n    return make('not-yet', null, 'last-progress-at', 'no target-owned work-item progress is visible yet');\n  }\n  const activityMs = input.reading.lastProgressAt instanceof Date\n    ? input.reading.lastProgressAt.getTime()\n    : Date.parse(input.reading.lastProgressAt);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(activityMs)) {\n    return make('unreadable', null, 'last-progress-at', 'target work-item progress timestamp is unreadable; measurement failed');\n  }\n  const activityAt = new Date(activityMs).toISOString();\n  if (activityMs > deliveredMs) {\n    return make('confirmed', activityAt, 'last-progress-at', 'the target recorded work-item progress strictly after recovery delivery');\n  }\n  return make('not-yet', activityAt, 'last-progress-at', 'the target work-item progress predates recovery delivery; pickup remains unconfirmed');\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "packages/operator-core/lib/work-item-admission-recovery.ts",
      "sha256": "21ca16967530c2d14c9385331f9ebb5266a8b2d2a257645e460390f3dbafb9f7",
      "excerptStartLine": 392,
      "excerptEndLine": 412,
      "excerpt": "\n/** Re-read the target's own item progress after delivery and persist a request-bound\n * three-valued receipt. Identical evidence is idempotent; only confirmed advances stage. */\nexport async function observeAdmissionRecoveryPickup(\n  args: AdmissionRecoveryScope & { requestId: string; target: string; nowMs?: number },\n  deps: { readActivity?: AdmissionRecoveryActivityReader } = {},\n  sql: Sql = getOrgPg().sql,\n): Promise<AdmissionRecoveryPickupObservation> {\n  const initial = await sql.begin(async (rawTx) => {\n    const tx = rawTx as unknown as Sql;\n    const item = await readItem(tx, args);\n    const request = savedRequest(item);\n    if (!request || request.requestId !== args.requestId || request.target !== args.target) {\n      throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('pickup observation does not match the saved recovery request and target');\n    }\n    await verifyRequest(tx, args, request);\n    assertPickupObservationIdentity(args, request);\n    if (request.state === 'delivered' && request.stage === 'picked-up') {\n      return { request, alreadyConfirmed: request.pickupObservation! };\n    }\n    if (request.state !== 'delivered' || request.stage !== 'await-pickup') {\n"
    },

      "path": "packages/operator-core/lib/work-item-admission-recovery.ts",
      "sha256": "21ca16967530c2d14c9385331f9ebb5266a8b2d2a257645e460390f3dbafb9f7",
      "excerptStartLine": 477,
      "excerptEndLine": 548,
      "excerpt": "export async function requestAdmissionRecovery(\n  input: AdmissionRecoveryScope & { caller: string; target: string; note?: string; body?: string; authority?: AdmissionRecoveryAuthority },\n  deps: Pick<AdmissionRecoveryDeps, 'authorize'> = { authorize: authorizeAdmissionRecovery },\n  sql: Sql = getOrgPg().sql,\n): Promise<{ ok: true; workItemId: string; request: AdmissionRecoveryRequest; inProgress: boolean }> {\n  if (![input.workspaceId, input.harnessSlug, input.workItemId, input.caller, input.target].every((s) => s.trim())) {\n    throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('recovery requires exact workspace, harness, item, caller, and target identities');\n  }\n  await deps.authorize(input, input);\n  return sql.begin(async (rawTx) => {\n    const tx = rawTx as unknown as Sql;\n    const locked = await lockRequest(tx, input);\n    const item = await healOwnNodeStrand(tx, input, await readItem(tx, input));\n    assertItemEligible(item, input.target);\n    const rawPrevious = item.payload?._admissionRecovery;\n    // This is a NEW authorized request, not replay by the routine. A rotated key or\n    // malformed public payload must not permanently wedge the original item.\n    // Well-formed requests retain their exact identity and actor binding; only this\n    // fresh authorization path may re-sign them with the current key.\n    const parsed = requestSchema.safeParse(rawPrevious);\n    const previous = parsed.success ? parsed.data : null;\n    if (previous && (previous.caller !== input.caller || previous.target !== input.target)) {\n      throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused(`existing recovery belongs to ${previous.caller} for ${previous.target}`);\n    }\n    if (!locked) {\n      if (!previous) throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('original-item recovery is being registered; retry this same item');\n      if ((input.note !== undefined && input.note !== previous.note) || (input.body !== undefined && input.body !== previous.body)) {\n        throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('original recovery is in progress; retry this same item to update its instructions');\n      }\n      return { ok: true as const, workItemId: input.workItemId, request: previous, inProgress: true };\n    }\n    const routineId = await assertRoutineActive(tx, input, previous?.routineId);\n    // Recheck after serialization too: a retry is not cached permission.\n    await deps.authorize(input, input);\n    const now = new Date().toISOString();\n    const unsigned: Omit<AdmissionRecoveryRequest, 'signature'> = {\n      version: input.authority ? 3 : 2, requestId: previous?.requestId ?? randomUUID(), caller: input.caller, target: input.target,\n      routineId, requestedAt: previous?.requestedAt ?? now, updatedAt: now,\n      state: 'queued', stage: 'queued', pickupConfirmed: false,\n      ...(input.authority ? { authority: input.authority } : {}),\n      ...(input.note !== undefined || previous?.note !== undefined ? { note: input.note ?? previous?.note } : {}),\n      ...(input.body !== undefined || previous?.body !== undefined ? { body: input.body ?? previous?.body } : {}),\n    };\n    const request = requestSchema.parse({ ...unsigned, signature: await requestSignature(tx, input, unsigned) });\n    if (!await writeRequest(tx, input, request, rawPrevious)) {\n      throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('original recovery changed during registration; retry this same item');\n    }\n    if (request.state === 'queued') {\n      // Acceleration must retain the natural ordinary deadline. Otherwise each\n      // queued exact request can consume another recurring ordinary opportunity.\n      const updated = await tx`\n        UPDATE harness_shared.routines\n           SET metadata = jsonb_set(COALESCE(metadata, '{}'::jsonb), '{admissionOrdinarySlot}',\n                 COALESCE(metadata->'admissionOrdinarySlot',\n                   jsonb_build_object('dueAt', COALESCE(next_fire_at, now())))),\n               next_fire_at = LEAST(COALESCE(next_fire_at, now()), now())\n         WHERE id = ${routineId} AND workspace_id = ${input.workspaceId}\n           AND install_slug = ${input.harnessSlug} AND name = ${WORK_ITEM_ADMISSION_PROMOTER}\n           AND target_role = ${`system:${WORK_ITEM_ADMISSION_PROMOTER}`} AND active = true\n           AND jsonb_typeof(COALESCE(metadata, '{}'::jsonb)) = 'object'\n         RETURNING id`;\n      if (updated.length !== 1) throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('promoter was paused during recovery registration');\n    }\n    return { ok: true as const, workItemId: input.workItemId, request, inProgress: false };\n  });\n}\n\n/** Serialize the ordinary/recovery choice without holding a transaction across\n * model I/O. A later workflow can resume a stranded slot's original run id. */\nexport async function withAdmissionPromoterLock<T>(\n  scope: Pick<AdmissionRecoveryScope, 'workspaceId' | 'harnessSlug'>,\n  lockSql: Sql | undefined,\n"

      "excerpt": "/** Ordering hints for the ordinary component writer, never replay/assignment grants.\n * Exact-request fires still stop at their original mutation scope. */\nexport async function readAuthorizedComponentScreening(\n  scope: Omit<AdmissionRecoveryScope, 'workItemId'>,\n  deps: Pick<AdmissionRecoveryDeps, 'authorize'>,\n  sql: Sql = getOrgPg().sql,\n): Promise<ComponentScreeningSelection> {\n  // Advance the bounded window even when every candidate fails eligibility.\n  // Otherwise old reviewed, malformed or no-longer-authorized requests can\n  // permanently hide later requests. Reuse the owning routine's metadata;\n  // neither admission nor the signed request is changed by this scan cursor.\n  const rows = await sql.begin(async (tx): Promise<{ feature_id: string }[]> => {\n    const [routine] = await tx<{ id: string; cursor: string | null }[]>`\n      SELECT id, metadata #>> '{admissionComponentCursor}' AS cursor\n        FROM harness_shared.routines\n       WHERE workspace_id = ${scope.workspaceId} AND install_slug = ${scope.harnessSlug}\n         AND name = ${WORK_ITEM_ADMISSION_PROMOTER}\n         AND target_role = ${`system:${WORK_ITEM_ADMISSION_PROMOTER}`} AND active = true\n       FOR UPDATE`;\n    if (!routine) return [];\n    const selected = await tx<{ feature_id: string }[]>`\n      SELECT feature_id FROM harness_shared.work_items\n       WHERE workspace_id = ${scope.workspaceId} AND harness_slug = ${scope.harnessSlug}\n         AND admission IN ('pending', 'unreviewed')\n         AND payload #>> '{_admissionRecovery,state}' = 'held'\n         AND payload #>> '{_admissionRecovery,stage}' = 'screen'\n         AND payload #>> '{_admissionRecovery,version}' = '3'\n         AND position('outside-mutation-scope' IN COALESCE(payload #>> '{_admissionRecovery,reason}', '')) > 0\n       ORDER BY (feature_id > ${routine.cursor ?? ''}) DESC, feature_id LIMIT 20`;\n    if (selected.length > 0) {\n      await tx`UPDATE harness_shared.routines\n         SET metadata = jsonb_set(COALESCE(metadata, '{}'::jsonb), '{admissionComponentCursor}',\n               to_jsonb(${selected[selected.length - 1]!.feature_id}::text))\n       WHERE id = ${routine.id} AND workspace_id = ${scope.workspaceId}\n         AND install_slug = ${scope.harnessSlug}`;\n    }\n    return Array.from(selected);\n  });\n  const ids: string[] = [];\n  const receipts: ComponentScreeningReceipt[] = [];\n  for (const row of rows) {\n    const exact = { ...scope, workItemId: row.feature_id };\n    let stage: ComponentScreeningReceipt['stage'] = 'request';\n    const record = (reason: ComponentScreeningReceipt['reason']) => {\n      receipts.push({ itemId: row.feature_id, stage, reason });\n    };\n    try {\n      const item = await readItem(sql, exact);\n      const request = savedRequest(item);\n      if (!request || request.version !== 3 || request.state !== 'held' || request.stage !== 'screen' ||\n          !request.reason?.includes('outside-mutation-scope')) {\n        record('request-ineligible');\n        continue;\n      }\n      // A held request gets one ordinary adjudication opportunity. A structural\n      // prescreen hold did not judge its protected twin, so it cannot consume\n      // that opportunity. readPromoterWindow defers these priority rows without\n      // a model slot while a twin remains protected.\n      const review = item.payload?.admissionReview as { lastRetryAt?: string } | undefined;\n      const hold = item.payload?.admissionHold as {\n        heldAt?: string; actor?: string; reason?: string; screeningDeferred?: boolean;\n      } | undefined;\n      const requestedScreenAt = Date.parse(request.updatedAt);\n      const heldAt = Date.parse(hold?.heldAt ?? '');\n      const retryAt = Date.parse(review?.lastRetryAt ?? '');\n      // Existing receipts predate the typed marker. Recognize only the exact\n      // system-generated legacy format; an explicit false always wins.\n      const screeningDeferred = hold?.actor === 'system:work-item-admission-promoter' &&\n        (hold.screeningDeferred === true || hold.screeningDeferred === undefined &&\n          /^deferred: twin \\S+ is [a-z-]+; held until it can be judged$/.test(hold.reason ?? ''));\n      stage = 'freshness';\n      if (!Number.isFinite(requestedScreenAt)) { record('screen-time-invalid'); continue; }\n      if (heldAt >= requestedScreenAt && !screeningDeferred) { record('already-held'); continue; }\n      if (retryAt >= requestedScreenAt && !(screeningDeferred && retryAt === heldAt)) {\n        record('already-reviewed'); continue;\n      }\n      stage = 'provenance';\n      await verifyRequest(sql, exact, request);\n      stage = 'routine';\n      await assertRoutineActive(sql, exact, request.routineId);\n      stage = 'eligibility';\n      assertItemEligible(item, request.target);\n      stage = 'authority';\n      await deps.authorize(exact, request);\n      // Authorization may wait on policy I/O. Do not prioritize a replaced request\n      // or an item newly held by a peer during that read.\n      stage = 'recheck';\n      const current = await readItem(sql, exact);\n      assertItemEligible(current, request.target);\n      const [same] = await sql<{ unchanged: boolean }[]>`\n        SELECT payload->'_admissionRecovery' = ${sql.typed(JSON.stringify(item.payload?._admissionRecovery), 25)}::jsonb AS unchanged\n          FROM harness_shared.work_items WHERE workspace_id = ${exact.workspaceId}\n            AND harness_slug = ${exact.harnessSlug} AND feature_id = ${exact.workItemId}`;\n      if (same?.unchanged) {\n        ids.push(row.feature_id);\n        record('selected');\n      } else record('request-changed');\n    } catch (error) {\n      if (!(error instanceof AdmissionRecoveryRefused) && !(error instanceof AdmissionAuthorityRefused)) throw error;\n      // An invalid request earns no priority. Ordinary screening and its own\n      // endpoint protections remain available without mutating the saved request.\n      record(error instanceof AdmissionAuthorityRefused ? 'authority-refused' : 'recovery-refused');\n    }\n  }\n  return { itemIds: ids, receipts };\n"

      "excerptStartLine": 731,

      "excerpt": "  const rows = await sql<{ feature_id: string; queued: boolean }[]>`\n    SELECT feature_id, payload->'_admissionRecovery'->>'state' = 'queued' AS queued\n      FROM harness_shared.work_items\n     WHERE workspace_id = ${recoveryScope.workspaceId} AND harness_slug = ${recoveryScope.harnessSlug}\n       AND NOT (feature_id = ANY(${[...excludedItemIds]}::text[]))\n       AND (\n         payload->'_admissionRecovery'->>'state' = 'queued'\n         OR (payload->'_admissionRecovery'->>'state' = 'delivered'\n           AND payload->'_admissionRecovery'->>'stage' = 'await-pickup')\n       )\n     ORDER BY (payload->'_admissionRecovery'->>'state' = 'queued') DESC, feature_id LIMIT 20`;\n  if (rows.length === 0) return 0;\n  // Keep the request-accelerated write boundary even when another consumer owns\n  // a queued request's lock. Pickup-only observations must not starve normal\n  // screening forever, nor occupy the bounded window ahead of new requests.\n  const queued = new Set(rows.filter((row) => row.queued).map((row) => row.feature_id));\n\n  const ownsLockSql = deps.lockSql === undefined;\n  const lockSql = deps.lockSql ?? createAdmissionRecoveryLockSql();\n"

      "excerpt": "          } catch (error) {\n            if (!(error instanceof AdmissionRecoveryRefused)) throw error;\n            await sql.begin(async (rawTx) => {\n              const tx = rawTx as unknown as Sql;\n              await writeRequest(tx, exact, {\n                ...request, state: 'held', stage: 'await-pickup',\n                reason: error.message.slice(0, 2000), updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n              }, rawRequest);\n            });\n          }\n          continue;\n        }\n        const assertAllowed = async () => {\n          await verifyRequest(sql, exact, request);\n          await assertRoutineActive(sql, exact, request.routineId);\n          const live = await healOwnNodeStrand(sql, exact, await readItem(sql, exact));\n          const current = savedRequest(live);\n          // SQL jsonb equality ignores object-key ordering, unlike JSON.stringify.\n          const [same] = await sql<{ unchanged: boolean }[]>`\n            SELECT COALESCE(payload->'_admissionRecovery', 'null'::jsonb) =\n              ${sql.typed(JSON.stringify(rawRequest ?? null), 25)}::jsonb AS unchanged\n              FROM harness_shared.work_items WHERE workspace_id = ${exact.workspaceId}\n                AND harness_slug = ${exact.harnessSlug} AND feature_id = ${exact.workItemId}`;\n          if (!current || !same?.unchanged) {\n            throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('original recovery identity changed');\n          }\n          await verifyRequest(sql, exact, current);\n          assertItemEligible(live, request.target);\n          await deps.authorize(exact, request);\n        };\n        let stage: z.infer<typeof recoveryStageSchema> = 'authorize';\n        try {\n          await assertAllowed();\n          const item = await readItem(sql, exact);\n          let screening: Awaited<ReturnType<AdmissionRecoveryDeps['screen']>> = undefined;\n          if (item.admission === 'pending' || item.admission === 'unreviewed') {\n            stage = 'screen';\n            screening = await deps.screen(exact, assertAllowed);\n          }\n          await assertAllowed();\n          const screened = await readItem(sql, exact);\n          const refusals = screening?.guardRefusals?.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === exact.workItemId) ?? [];\n          const screeningGuard = refusals.length > 0\n            ? ` (admission guard from run ${screening!.runId}: ${refusals.map(({ reason, detail }) => `${reason}: ${detail}`).join('; ')})`\n            : '';\n          // Fail-open admission is not review clearance. The resume path also\n          // blocks this pending review, even if screening changed admission.\n          // Preserve scope refusals so the authenticated ordinary component\n          // selector can find this same request while its review is pending.\n          const review = screened.payload?.admissionReview as { state?: unknown } | undefined;\n          if (review?.state === 'pending') {\n            stage = 'screen';\n            throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused(\n              `original item admission review is pending${screeningGuard}; finish its ordinary admission review and retry this same item`,\n            );\n          }\n          if (screened.admission === 'pending') {\n            // Guarded component writes leave the rows unchanged, so their current\n            // refusal lives in the screening receipt rather than admissionHold.\n            const hold = screened.payload?.admissionHold as { reason?: unknown; runId?: unknown } | undefined;\n            const named = screeningGuard || (typeof hold?.reason === 'string' && hold.reason.trim()\n                ? ` (admission hold${typeof hold.runId === 'string' ? ` from run ${hold.runId}` : ''}: ${hold.reason.trim()})`\n                : ' (the promoter recorded no hold reason)');\n            const repair = refusals.some(({ reason }) => reason === 'outside-mutation-scope')\n              ? 'use the ordinary component screening/review route, then retry this same item within its authorized scope'\n              : 'repair its named admission hold and retry this same item';\n            throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused(\n              `screening left the original item pending${named}; ${repair}`,\n            );\n          }\n          stage = 'assign';\n          const result = await deps.dispatch({\n            workItemIds: [exact.workItemId], targetAgent: request.target,\n            harness: exact.harnessSlug, workspaceId: exact.workspaceId,\n            summary: request.note ?? 'Resume original work item ' + exact.workItemId + ' after admission recovery',\n            ...(request.body !== undefined ? { instructions: request.body } : {}),\n            source: 'system:work-item-admission-recovery',\n            beforeMutate: async (mutation) => {\n              stage = mutation === 'wake' ? 'wake' : 'assign';\n              await assertAllowed();\n            },\n          });\n          // Assignment and wake have their own durable writes. 'await-pickup' : result.wake ? 'wake' : 'assign';\n          const updated = await sql.begin(async (rawTx) => {\n            const tx = rawTx as unknown as Sql;\n            const current = await readItem(tx, exact);\n            const currentRequest = savedRequest(current);\n            const [same] = await tx<{ unchanged: boolean }[]>`\n              SELECT COALESCE(payload->'_admissionRecovery', 'null'::jsonb) =\n                ${tx.typed(JSON.stringify(rawRequest ?? null), 25)}::jsonb AS unchanged\n                FROM harness_shared.work_items WHERE workspace_id = ${exact.workspaceId}\n                  AND harness_slug = ${exact.harnessSlug} AND feature_id = ${exact.workItemId}`;\n            if (!currentRequest || !same?.unchanged) return false;\n            await verifyRequest(tx, exact, currentRequest);\n            await assertRoutineActive(tx, exact, request.routineId);\n            const updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();\n            const updatedRequest = {\n              ...request, state: result.ok && queued ? 'delivered' as const : 'held' as const, stage,\n              reason: result.assignment?.skipped[0]?.reason ?? result.assignment?.failed[0]?.reason ?? result.warning ??\n                (queued ? 'wake queued; target pickup remains unconfirmed' : 'dispatch did not queue a wake'),\n              updatedAt,\n            };\n            if (result.ok && queued) updatedRequest.deliveredAt = updatedAt;\n            else {\n              delete updatedRequest.deliveredAt;\n              delete updatedRequest.pickupObservation;\n            }\n            return writeRequest(tx, exact, updatedRequest, rawRequest);\n          });\n          if (!updated) throw new AdmissionRecoveryRefused('original recovery identity changed');\n        } catch (error) {\n          if (!(error instanceof AdmissionRecoveryRefused)) throw error;\n          await sql.begin(async (rawTx) => {\n            const tx = rawTx as unknown as Sql;\n            await writeRequest(tx, exact, {\n              ...request, state: 'held', stage,\n              reason: error.message.slice(0, 2000), updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n            }, rawRequest);\n          });\n        }\n      } finally {\n"

      "savedNovember7FloorVerdict": {
        "id": "EI-26151719139224586",
        "admissible": false,
        "refusedBy": "admission-pending",

        ],
        "detail": "duplicate screening is pending for this WORK-ITEM CLAIM. It does not block opening an agent or fleet process; launch and queue admission are separate capabilities. A launched agent still needs claimable or validly pre-assigned work. This row carries DUPLICATE CANDIDATES found when it was filed, at least one stronger than a soft semantic match, so only the promoter's model review can admit it (as distinct) or merge it into its twin. The promoter runs on a 30-minute tick but reviews only a few rows per tick, candidate rows first, so this wait has NO fixed bound; the independent fail-open runner deliberately never admits a row with unresolved strong candidates. It still CLEARS ON ITS OWN, and it is not a permanent refusal — but do not wait on it: check whether a named candidate is the same problem and work that row instead, or use a create-time admission route for genuinely distinct work.",
        "failOpenExclusion": "duplicate-candidates",
        "remedy": "Choose the work route before creating a replacement. Existing-plan fleet work should use plans:start then fleet:launch-on-plan; its promoted items use the plan-item bypass. Fixed ad-hoc work should be created atomically with assign_to naming the intended owner, then launched with capability:launch-agent. Any one of these makes a NEW item born admitted: the item implements a plan item; severity:'critical'; a 'security' topic; assign_to names the intended owner. These are create-time admission bypasses, not blanket authority: assign_to is still checked against the target's live fleet scope, dependencies, locks, security, credentials and resource admission; a scope mismatch files the item unassigned and explains why. Launching does not change an ALREADY-pending item. Wait for its promoter/backstop instead of duplicating or re-filing it; only the existing server-derived dispatch/force paths may grant their narrower exceptions."
      },
      "savedNovember7ObservedAt": "2026-11-07 11:32:12.960427-05",

      "savedNovember7FloorVerdict": {
        "id": "EI-26266665899666720",
        "admissible": false,
        "refusedBy": "admission-pending",
        "failedFloors": [
          "admission-pending",
          "admission-recovery",
          "admission-hold"
        ],
        "detail": "duplicate screening is pending for this WORK-ITEM CLAIM. It does not block opening an agent or fleet process; launch and queue admission are separate capabilities. A launched agent still needs claimable or validly pre-assigned work. This row carries DUPLICATE CANDIDATES found when it was filed, at least one stronger than a soft semantic match, so only the promoter's model review can admit it (as distinct) or merge it into its twin. The promoter runs on a 30-minute tick but reviews only a few rows per tick, candidate rows first, so this wait has NO fixed bound; the independent fail-open runner deliberately never admits a row with unresolved strong candidates. It still CLEARS ON ITS OWN, and it is not a permanent refusal — but do not wait on it: check whether a named candidate is the same problem and work that row instead, or use a create-time admission route for genuinely distinct work.",
        "failOpenExclusion": "duplicate-candidates",
        "remedy": "Choose the work route before creating a replacement. Existing-plan fleet work should use plans:start then fleet:launch-on-plan; its promoted items use the plan-item bypass. Fixed ad-hoc work should be created atomically with assign_to naming the intended owner, then launched with capability:launch-agent. Any one of these makes a NEW item born admitted: the item implements a plan item; severity:'critical'; a 'security' topic; assign_to names the intended owner. These are create-time admission bypasses, not blanket authority: assign_to is still checked against the target's live fleet scope, dependencies, locks, security, credentials and resource admission; a scope mismatch files the item unassigned and explains why. Launching does not change an ALREADY-pending item. Wait for its promoter/backstop instead of duplicating or re-filing it; only the existing server-derived dispatch/force paths may grant their narrower exceptions."
      },

        "detail": "duplicate screening is pending for this WORK-ITEM CLAIM. It does not block opening an agent or fleet process; launch and queue admission are separate capabilities. A launched agent still needs claimable or validly pre-assigned work. This row carries DUPLICATE CANDIDATES found when it was filed, at least one stronger than a soft semantic match, so only the promoter's model review can admit it (as distinct) or merge it into its twin. The promoter runs on a 30-minute tick but reviews only a few rows per tick, candidate rows first, so this wait has NO fixed bound; the independent fail-open runner deliberately never admits a row with unresolved strong candidates. It still CLEARS ON ITS OWN, and it is not a permanent refusal — but do not wait on it: check whether a named candidate is the same problem and work that row instead, or use a create-time admission route for genuinely distinct work.",
        "failOpenExclusion": "duplicate-candidates",
        "remedy": "Choose the work route before creating a replacement. Existing-plan fleet work should use plans:start then fleet:launch-on-plan; its promoted items use the plan-item bypass. Fixed ad-hoc work should be created atomically with assign_to naming the intended owner, then launched with capability:launch-agent. Any one of these makes a NEW item born admitted: the item implements a plan item; severity:'critical'; a 'security' topic; assign_to names the intended owner. These are create-time admission bypasses, not blanket authority: assign_to is still checked against the target's live fleet scope, dependencies, locks, security, credentials and resource admission; a scope mismatch files the item unassigned and explains why. Launching does not change an ALREADY-pending item. Wait for its promoter/backstop instead of duplicating or re-filing it; only the existing server-derived dispatch/force paths may grant their narrower exceptions."
      },

      "savedNovember7FloorVerdict": {
        "id": "EI-26658156767336740",
        "admissible": false,
        "refusedBy": "admission-pending",

          "admission-pending",
          "admission-recovery",
          "admission-hold"
        ],
        "detail": "duplicate screening is pending for this WORK-ITEM CLAIM. It does not block opening an agent or fleet process; launch and queue admission are separate capabilities. A launched agent still needs claimable or validly pre-assigned work. This row carries DUPLICATE CANDIDATES found when it was filed, at least one stronger than a soft semantic match, so only the promoter's model review can admit it (as distinct) or merge it into its twin. The promoter runs on a 30-minute tick but reviews only a few rows per tick, candidate rows first, so this wait has NO fixed bound; the independent fail-open runner deliberately never admits a row with unresolved strong candidates. It still CLEARS ON ITS OWN, and it is not a permanent refusal — but do not wait on it: check whether a named candidate is the same problem and work that row instead, or use a create-time admission route for genuinely distinct work.",
        "failOpenExclusion": "duplicate-candidates",
        "remedy": "Choose the work route before creating a replacement. Existing-plan fleet work should use plans:start then fleet:launch-on-plan; its promoted items use the plan-item bypass. Fixed ad-hoc work should be created atomically with assign_to naming the intended owner, then launched with capability:launch-agent. Any one of these makes a NEW item born admitted: the item implements a plan item; severity:'critical'; a 'security' topic; assign_to names the intended owner. These are create-time admission bypasses, not blanket authority: assign_to is still checked against the target's live fleet scope, dependencies, locks, security, credentials and resource admission; a scope mismatch files the item unassigned and explains why. Launching does not change an ALREADY-pending item. Wait for its promoter/backstop instead of duplicating or re-filing it; only the existing server-derived dispatch/force paths may grant their narrower exceptions."
      },
      "savedNovember7ObservedAt": "2026-11-07 11:32:12.960427-05",

      "savedNovember7FloorVerdict": {
        "id": "EI-26768249861954750",
        "admissible": false,
        "refusedBy": "admission-pending",
        "failedFloors": [
          "admission-pending",
          "admission-recovery",
          "admission-hold"
        ],
        "detail": "duplicate screening is pending for this WORK-ITEM CLAIM. It does not block opening an agent or fleet process; launch and queue admission are separate capabilities. A launched agent still needs claimable or validly pre-assigned work. This row carries DUPLICATE CANDIDATES found when it was filed, at least one stronger than a soft semantic match, so only the promoter's model review can admit it (as distinct) or merge it into its twin. The promoter runs on a 30-minute tick but reviews only a few rows per tick, candidate rows first, so this wait has NO fixed bound; the independent fail-open runner deliberately never admits a row with unresolved strong candidates. It still CLEARS ON ITS OWN, and it is not a permanent refusal — but do not wait on it: check whether a named candidate is the same problem and work that row instead, or use a create-time admission route for genuinely distinct work.",
        "failOpenExclusion": "duplicate-candidates",
        "remedy": "Choose the work route before creating a replacement. Existing-plan fleet work should use plans:start then fleet:launch-on-plan; its promoted items use the plan-item bypass. Fixed ad-hoc work should be created atomically with assign_to naming the intended owner, then launched with capability:launch-agent. Any one of these makes a NEW item born admitted: the item implements a plan item; severity:'critical'; a 'security' topic; assign_to names the intended owner. These are create-time admission bypasses, not blanket authority: assign_to is still checked against the target's live fleet scope, dependencies, locks, security, credentials and resource admission; a scope mismatch files the item unassigned and explains why. Launching does not change an ALREADY-pending item. Wait for its promoter/backstop instead of duplicating or re-filing it; only the existing server-derived dispatch/force paths may grant their narrower exceptions."
      },
      "savedNovember7ObservedAt": "2026-11-07 11:32:12.960427-05",

      "savedNovember7FloorVerdict": {
        "id": "EI-26894253831917953",
        "admissible": false,
        "refusedBy": "admission-pending",
        "failedFloors": [
          "admission-pending",
          "admission-recovery",
          "admission-hold"
        ],
        "detail": "duplicate screening is pending for this WORK-ITEM CLAIM. It does not block opening an agent or fleet process; launch and queue admission are separate capabilities. A launched agent still needs claimable or validly pre-assigned work. This row carries DUPLICATE CANDIDATES found when it was filed, at least one stronger than a soft semantic match, so only the promoter's model review can admit it (as distinct) or merge it into its twin. The promoter runs on a 30-minute tick but reviews only a few rows per tick, candidate rows first, so this wait has NO fixed bound; the independent fail-open runner deliberately never admits a row with unresolved strong candidates. It still CLEARS ON ITS OWN, and it is not a permanent refusal — but do not wait on it: check whether a named candidate is the same problem and work that row instead, or use a create-time admission route for genuinely distinct work.",
        "failOpenExclusion": "duplicate-candidates",
        "remedy": "Choose the work route before creating a replacement. Existing-plan fleet work should use plans:start then fleet:launch-on-plan; its promoted items use the plan-item bypass. Fixed ad-hoc work should be created atomically with assign_to naming the intended owner, then launched with capability:launch-agent. Any one of these makes a NEW item born admitted: the item implements a plan item; severity:'critical'; a 'security' topic; assign_to names the intended owner. These are create-time admission bypasses, not blanket authority: assign_to is still checked against the target's live fleet scope, dependencies, locks, security, credentials and resource admission; a scope mismatch files the item unassigned and explains why. Launching does not change an ALREADY-pending item. Wait for its promoter/backstop instead of duplicating or re-filing it; only the existing server-derived dispatch/force paths may grant their narrower exceptions."
      },

        "id": "EI-27017692842649485",

        "detail": "duplicate screening is pending for this WORK-ITEM CLAIM. It does not block opening an agent or fleet process; launch and queue admission are separate capabilities. A launched agent still needs claimable or validly pre-assigned work. This row carries DUPLICATE CANDIDATES found when it was filed, at least one stronger than a soft semantic match, so only the promoter's model review can admit it (as distinct) or merge it into its twin. The promoter runs on a 30-minute tick but reviews only a few rows per tick, candidate rows first, so this wait has NO fixed bound; the independent fail-open runner deliberately never admits a row with unresolved strong candidates. It still CLEARS ON ITS OWN, and it is not a permanent refusal — but do not wait on it: check whether a named candidate is the same problem and work that row instead, or use a create-time admission route for genuinely distinct work.",
        "failOpenExclusion": "duplicate-candidates",
        "remedy": "Choose the work route before creating a replacement. Existing-plan fleet work should use plans:start then fleet:launch-on-plan; its promoted items use the plan-item bypass. Fixed ad-hoc work should be created atomically with assign_to naming the intended owner, then launched with capability:launch-agent. Any one of these makes a NEW item born admitted: the item implements a plan item; severity:'critical'; a 'security' topic; assign_to names the intended owner. These are create-time admission bypasses, not blanket authority: assign_to is still checked against the target's live fleet scope, dependencies, locks, security, credentials and resource admission; a scope mismatch files the item unassigned and explains why. Launching does not change an ALREADY-pending item. Wait for its promoter/backstop instead of duplicating or re-filing it; only the existing server-derived dispatch/force paths may grant their narrower exceptions."
      },

Launching does not change an ALREADY-pending item. Wait for its promoter/backstop instead of duplicating or re-filing it; only the existing server-derived dispatch/force paths may grant their narrower exceptions."
      },

        "detail": "duplicate screening is pending for this WORK-ITEM CLAIM. It does not block opening an agent or fleet process; launch and queue admission are separate capabilities. A launched agent still needs claimable or validly pre-assigned work. This row carries DUPLICATE CANDIDATES found when it was filed, at least one stronger than a soft semantic match, so only the promoter's model review can admit it (as distinct) or merge it into its twin. The promoter runs on a 30-minute tick but reviews only a few rows per tick, candidate rows first, so this wait has NO fixed bound; the independent fail-open runner deliberately never admits a row with unresolved strong candidates. It still CLEARS ON ITS OWN, and it is not a permanent refusal — but do not wait on it: check whether a named candidate is the same problem and work that row instead, or use a create-time admission route for genuinely distinct work.",
        "failOpenExclusion": "duplicate-candidates",
        "remedy": "Choose the work route before creating a replacement. Existing-plan fleet work should use plans:start then fleet:launch-on-plan; its promoted items use the plan-item bypass. Fixed ad-hoc work should be created atomically with assign_to naming the intended owner, then launched with capability:launch-agent. Any one of these makes a NEW item born admitted: the item implements a plan item; severity:'critical'; a 'security' topic; assign_to names the intended owner. These are create-time admission bypasses, not blanket authority: assign_to is still checked against the target's live fleet scope, dependencies, locks, security, credentials and resource admission; a scope mismatch files the item unassigned and explains why. Launching does not change an ALREADY-pending item. Wait for its promoter/backstop instead of duplicating or re-filing it; only the existing server-derived dispatch/force paths may grant their narrower exceptions."
      },
